	gui:TweenPosition(gui.Position + UDim2.new(1,0,0,0),"Out", "Quad", .6)

coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
	while mainframe ~= nil do
		for _,bg in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
			if bg.Name == "BGFrame" then
				local basecolor = {(100 + (bg.Position.Y.Scale * 60))/255,(160 + (bg.Position.Y.Scale * 60))/255,1} --with possible offset of 13
				local newcolor = Color3.new(bg.BackgroundColor3.r + math.random(-1,1)/255,bg.BackgroundColor3.g + math.random(-1,1)/255,1)
				if newcolor.r > basecolor[1] - 15/255 and newcolor.r < basecolor[1] + 15/255 and newcolor.g > basecolor[2] - 15/255 and newcolor.g < basecolor[2] + 15/255 then
					bg.BackgroundColor3 = newcolor
				end
			end
		end
		wait(1/30)
	end
end))

function Check()
	local id = script.Parent.Text:match("%d+")
	if id then
		script.Parent.Text = id

		script.Parent.Parent.FavButton.ImageTransparency = 1
	end
end

=== chunk=419 type=PROP offset=219448 runSha256=ad3fbde1313ed4f953e8a7a0bd864665bf7d24dfe9eb40e5fe8e4aad0d364f8d ===
local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local shopframe = script.Parent:WaitForChild("Shop")
local frame = shopframe:WaitForChild("Items")
local queueframe = shopframe:WaitForChild("QueueFrame")

local queue = game.ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("SongQueueItems")

local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(plr.userId)
shopframe:WaitForChild("NumFavorites").Value = #data.SongLists.Favorites:GetChildren()

local unlockframe = shopframe:WaitForChild("UnlockFeaturesFrame")

local module = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.InterfaceModule)
module.openWindow(script.Parent.Shop)

local char = plr.Character
local torso
if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
	torso = char.HumanoidRootPart
	shopframe.ShopPos.Value = char.HumanoidRootPart.Position
end

function clearAllButtons()
	for i,v in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	frame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
end

function createButton(obj)
	local button = frame.BaseButton:clone()
	local num = #frame:GetChildren() - 1
	button.Name = obj.Name --the numerical ID
	button.SongName.Text = "Song ID: "..obj.Name.." [Loading...]"
	if data.SongLists.Favorites:FindFirstChild(obj.Name) then
		--is favorited
		button.FavButton.ImageTransparency = 0
		button.Favorited.Value = true
	end
	button.Position = button.Position + UDim2.new(0,0,0,36*num)
	button.Visible = true
	button.Parent = frame
	frame.CanvasSize = frame.CanvasSize + UDim2.new(0,0,0,36)
	if obj.Value == "" then
		local name
		pcall(function()
			local asset = game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):GetProductInfo(tonumber(obj.Name))
			if asset then
				name = asset.Name
			end
		end)
		if name then
			obj.Value = name
		else
			obj.Value = "NULL"
		end
	end
	if obj and obj.Value ~= "NULL" and button and button:FindFirstChild("SongName") then
		button.SongName.Text = obj.Value
	end
end


function setUpQueueInterface()
	for i,v in pairs(queueframe:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name == "QueueItem" then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	for i,queueitem in pairs(queue:GetChildren()) do
		local label = queueframe.BaseQueueItem:clone()
		label.TextLabel.Text = (i == 1 and "Now: " or (i..". "))..queueitem.Name
		label.SongName.Value = (i == 1 and "Now: " or (i..". "))..queueitem.Name
		label.SongID.Value = tonumber(queueitem:WaitForChild("SongID").Value)
		if data.SongLists.Favorites:FindFirstChild(label.SongID.Value) then
		--is favorited
			label.FavButton.ImageTransparency = 0
			label.Favorited.Value = true
		end
		label.Player.Value = queueitem:WaitForChild("PlayerName").Value
		label.Position = label.Position + UDim2.new(0,0,0,22 * (#queueframe:GetChildren() - 1))
		label.Name = "QueueItem"
		label.Parent = queueframe
		label.Visible = true
	end
end


shopframe:WaitForChild("LoadMenu").Event:connect(function(category, canvaspos)
	unlockframe.Visible = false
	frame.Visible = false
	queueframe.Visible = false
	
	shopframe.Category.Value = category
	
	for _,v in pairs(shopframe.NavBar:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name == category then
			v.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(193, 41, 44)
			v.TextStrokeTransparency = .5
			if category == "Favorites" then
				v.Text = "Favorites ("..shopframe.NumFavorites.Value.."/100)"
			elseif category == "Song Queue" then
				v.Text = "Song Queue ("..#game.ReplicatedStorage.SongQueueItems:GetChildren().."/10)"
			end
		else
			v.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(140, 22, 24)
			v.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
			v.Text = v.Name
		end
	end
	
	if category == "Favorites" or category == "Recently Played" then
		if game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.LocalGamePassCheck:Invoke("UnrestrictedDJ") == true then
			--has gamepass, display appropriate menus
			local list
			
			clearAllButtons()
			frame.Visible = true
			
			if category == "Favorites" then
				list = data.SongLists.Favorites
			else
				list = data.SongLists.RecentlyPlayed
			end
			
			for _,v in pairs(list:GetChildren()) do
				createButton(v)
			end
			if canvaspos then
				frame.CanvasPosition = canvaspos
			end
		else
			unlockframe.Visible = true
		end
	elseif category == "Song Queue" then
		queueframe.Visible = true
		setUpQueueInterface()
	end
end)

shopframe:WaitForChild("DisplayFailure").Event:connect(function(msg)
	shopframe.FailureWindow.Reason.Text = msg
	shopframe.FailureWindow.Visible = true
end)


-- Do effects for when song box is selected/unselected

local selectedbutton = shopframe:WaitForChild("SelectedButton")

function ChangeSelectedButton(button)
	if selectedbutton.Value and selectedbutton.Value.Parent ~= nil then
		selectedbutton.Value.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 16)
		selectedbutton.Value:WaitForChild("SongName").TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
	end
	button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(156, 156, 156)
	button:WaitForChild("SongName").TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 217, 0)
	selectedbutton.Value = button
end

-- Add the ID of this button's song to the input box

shopframe.SetInputBoxID.Event:connect(function(button)
	shopframe.AudioIDSelector.InputBox.Text = button.Name
	local name = button.SongName.Text
	if #name > 40 then
		name = string.sub(name,1,40).."..."
	end
	shopframe.AudioIDSelector.NoticeText.Text = "'"..name.."' selected. Add it to the queue!"
	ChangeSelectedButton(button)
end)

-- Toggle this song ID as a favorite

shopframe.ToggleFavorite.Event:connect(function(button)
	local id
	--check if they have unrestricted DJ
	if game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.LocalGamePassCheck:Invoke("UnrestrictedDJ") == false then
		shopframe.DisplayFailure:Fire("You must have Unrestricted DJ Powers to add songs to your Favorites list.")
		return
	end
	
	if button.Parent.Name == "AudioIDSelector" then
		id = button.Parent.InputBox.Text
		if not id:match("%d+") then return end
	elseif button.Parent.Name == "QueueItem" then
		id = tostring(button.Parent.SongID.Value)
	else
		id = button.Parent.Name
	end
	
	if button.Parent.Favorited.Value == false then
		if shopframe.NumFavorites.Value >= 100 then
			shopframe.DisplayFailure:Fire("Sorry, you have reached the maximum number of favorite songs.")
			return
		else
			shopframe.NumFavorites.Value = shopframe.NumFavorites.Value + 1
		end
	else
		shopframe.NumFavorites.Value = shopframe.NumFavorites.Value - 1
	end
	
	if shopframe.Category.Value == "Favorites" then
		shopframe.NavBar.Favorites.Text = "Favorites ("..shopframe.NumFavorites.Value.."/100)"
	end
	
	button.Parent.Favorited.Value = not button.Parent.Favorited.Value
	button.ImageTransparency = button.Parent.Favorited.Value and 0 or 1
	game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ToggleFavoriteSong:FireServer(id)
	
	if button.Parent.Name == "AudioIDSelector" then
		button.Parent.NoticeText.Text = "Song ID "..id.." ".. (button.Parent.Favorited.Value and "added to" or "removed from") .." favorites".. (button.Parent.Favorited.Value and "!" or ".")
		if shopframe.Category.Value == "Favorites" then
			local i = 0
			repeat
				i = i + 1
				wait(1/15)
			until i >= 10 or shopframe.Category.Value ~= "Favorites" or #data.SongLists.Favorites:GetChildren() == shopframe.NumFavorites.Value
			if shopframe.Category.Value == "Favorites" then
				shopframe.LoadMenu:Fire("Favorites", frame.CanvasPosition)
			end
		end
	end
	
end)

-- When "Add to Queue" button is clicked, submit the request and handle the result
-- (True = song added to queue. False w/ no reason = dont display error. False w/ reason = pop up window with reason. True w/ reason = song added, but it is past the 2 minute threshold and user doesn't have UDJ.)

shopframe:WaitForChild("AudioIDSelector"):WaitForChild("PlayButton").MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
	local id = shopframe.AudioIDSelector.InputBox.Text:match("%d+")
	if not id then
		shopframe.DisplayFailure:Fire("Please enter a valid Audio ID into the box.")
		return
	end
	
	shopframe.AudioIDSelector.NoticeText.Text = "Submitting..."
	local result, reason = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.RequestDJSong:InvokeServer(id)
	shopframe.AudioIDSelector.NoticeText.Text = ""
	if not result then
		if reason then
			shopframe.DisplayFailure:Fire(reason)
		end
	elseif result == true then
		shopframe.AudioIDSelector.InputBox.Text = " Enter audio ID..."
		shopframe.AudioIDSelector.Favorited.Value = false
		shopframe.AudioIDSelector.FavButton.ImageTransparency = 1
		shopframe.AudioIDSelector.NoticeText.Text = "Song added to queue!"
		if reason then
			--tell user that full length of song will not be played.
			local m = tostring(math.floor(reason / 60))
			local s = tostring(reason % 60)
			if #s == 1 then
				s = "0"..s
			end
			shopframe.SongLengthWindow.Reason.Text = "Song added to queue.\nOnly the first 2 minutes of the song will be played; to play the full-length song ("..m..":"..s.."), purchase Unrestricted DJ Powers!"
		end
	end
	shopframe.LoadMenu:Fire(shopframe.Category.Value, frame.CanvasPosition)
end)

-- Display help window when button is clicked.

local helpbutton = shopframe.AudioIDSelector:WaitForChild("HelpButton")

helpbutton.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()

game:GetService("MarketplaceService").PromptPurchaseFinished:connect(function(player, assetid, ispurchased)
	if player == plr and assetid == game.ReplicatedStorage.GamePasses.UnrestrictedDJ.Value and ispurchased then
		shopframe.ProcessingWindow.Visible = true
		local i = 0
		repeat wait(1/10) i = i + 1 until game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.LocalGamePassCheck:Invoke("UnrestrictedDJ") == true or i >= 80
		shopframe.ProcessingWindow.Cancel.Visible = true
		if i >= 80 then
			--not processed yet
			shopframe.ProcessingWindow.Header.Text = "Rejoin Server"
			shopframe.ProcessingWindow.Reason.Text = "Your purchase succeeded, but this server has not received the result yet. Try joining a different server if it doesn't work."
		else
			--success
			shopframe.ProcessingWindow.Header.Text = "Success!"
			shopframe.ProcessingWindow.Reason.Text = "Thank you for your purchase of Unrestricted DJ Powers!"
		end
		shopframe.LoadMenu:Fire(shopframe.Category.Value, frame.CanvasPosition)
	end
end)

	setUpQueueInterface()
end)

	setUpQueueInterface()
end)

	end

	gui.Desc.ItemName.Text = itemname

			gui.Desc.ItemCostDiscounted.Text = asset.PriceInRobux.." R$"
			gui.Desc.Savings.Text = "You save: "..baseprice - asset.PriceInRobux.." R$!"

			gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextTransparency = .5
		else
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: "..asset.PriceInRobux.." R$"
			gui.Desc.DiscountSlash.Visible = false
			gui.Desc.ItemCostDiscounted.Text = ""
			gui.Desc.Savings.Text = ""
			
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextStrokeTransparency = .7
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextTransparency = 0
		end

		gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "(failed to load price)"
	end

	hoverimg.Visible = false

local shopname = script.Parent.Name
if shopname:match("ACTIVE_") then
	shopname = string.sub(shopname,8)
end
local category = script.Parent:WaitForChild("ItemCategory") --the category of perma-item that should be loaded (pets, gear, etc.)

local shop = {} --table referencing all the objects that belong to the category

for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
	if v.Name:match(category.Value.."_") then
		table.insert(shop,v)
	end
end


local currenttime = os.time()
local sale_end_time = 1482444000
local oldcostname = "BoomboxOldCost" --for showing discounts for certain items in future?


local gui = script.Parent:WaitForChild("Frame")
local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local enabled = gui:WaitForChild("Enabled")

local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(plr.userId)

gui:WaitForChild("Header").Text = shopname

local details = game.ReplicatedStorage.PermaShopDetails:FindFirstChild(shopname)
if details then
	gui:WaitForChild("SubHeader").Text = details.SubHeader.Value
end

--now load all the buttons
local xv = 0
local yv = 0

for i,v in pairs(shop) do
	if not v:FindFirstChild("NotForSale") and not (v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and v.ReleaseTime.Value > game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value and game.PlaceId == 9689581) and not (v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") and v.TimeToGoOffSale.Value < game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value) then
		local button = gui.Items.BaseButton:clone() --make new button for each item
		button.Parent = gui.Items
		button.Name = v.Name
		button.ItemID.Value = v.ItemID.Value
		button.Image = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id="..v.DecalID.Value
		button.Position = UDim2.new(0, xv*75, 0, yv*75)
		if v:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") then
			local bird = script.TwitterBird:clone()
			bird.Parent = button
			bird.Visible = true
		end
		local oldcost = v:FindFirstChild(oldcostname)
		if oldcost and currenttime < sale_end_time and not data.Objects:FindFirstChild(v.ItemID.Value) then
			local percent = math.ceil(-(v.ItemCost.Value - oldcost.Value) / oldcost.Value * 100)
			if percent == 34 then
				percent = 33
			end
			local oc = Instance.new("IntValue")
			oc.Name = "LocalOldCost"
			oc.Value = oldcost.Value
			oc.Parent = button
			button.DiscountPercent.Text = percent.."%"
			button.DiscountBanner.Visible = true
		end
		if v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*21 and not v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") then
			local newb = script.NewBanner:clone()
			newb.Parent = button
			newb.Visible = true
		elseif v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") then
			local b = script.LimitedBanner:clone()
			b.Parent = button
			b.Visible = true
			local offsaletime = Instance.new("IntValue")
			offsaletime.Name = "OffSaleTime"
			offsaletime.Value = v.TimeToGoOffSale.Value
			offsaletime.Parent = button
		end
		xv = xv + 1
		if xv == 4 then
			xv = 0
			yv = yv + 1
		end
		button.Visible = true
	end
end

gui.Items.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,(yv + 1) * 75)

module.openWindow(gui)

local torso
if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
	torso = char.HumanoidRootPart

function setButtonColors()
	for _,button in pairs(gui.Items:GetChildren()) do
		if button.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
			if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(button.ItemID.Value) then --has object
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.5,1,.5)
			else
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
			end
			if gui.SelectedItem.Value == button.ItemID.Value then
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,.25)
			end
		end
	end
end


function displayFailureWindow(result,reason) --reason == amount of cash needed
	if result == false then
		
		local failure = gui.FailureWindow
		failure.ProductId.Value = 0
		failure.Reason.Text = reason
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = true
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = false
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	elseif result == "cash" then
		
		local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
		local cash = data.cash.Value
		local products = game.ReplicatedStorage.CashProducts:GetChildren()
		local bestprod
		local highestprod
		for i,v in pairs(products) do
			if v.Name ~= "30" then
				local diff = tonumber(v.Name) - reason
				if diff >= 0 and (bestprod == nil or tonumber(bestprod.Name) > tonumber(v.Name)) then
					bestprod = v
				end
				if highestprod == nil or tonumber(v.Name) > tonumber(highestprod.Name) then
					highestprod = v
				end
			end
		end
		if bestprod == nil then
			bestprod = highestprod
		end
		
		local failure = gui.FailureWindow
		failure.Reason.Text = "You don't have enough Cash to buy this item. Would you like to get more Cash?"
		failure.CashAmount.Value = tonumber(bestprod.Name)
		failure.ProductId.Value = bestprod.Value
		failure.BuyCashButtons.GetCash.Text = "Get +"..bestprod.Name.." Cash!"
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = false
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = true
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	end
end


gui.BuyRequest.Event:connect(function(button)
	if enabled.Value == true then
		enabled.Value = false
		local oldbg = button.BackgroundColor3
		local oldborder = button.BorderColor3
		button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.6,.6,.6)
		button.BorderColor3 = Color3.new(.1,.1,.1)
		local result,reason = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PurchasePermanentItem:InvokeServer( gui.SelectedItem.Value) --the item ID
		if result == true then
			local sound = Instance.new("Sound",plr.PlayerGui)
			sound.Name = "ShopBuySuccess"
			sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..game.ReplicatedStorage.AssetIDs[sound.Name].Value
			sound:Play()
			game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,3)
		else
			displayFailureWindow(result,reason)
		end
		button.BackgroundColor3 = oldbg
		button.BorderColor3 = oldborder
		enabled.Value = true
	end
end)

gui.LoadMenu.Event:connect(function(button) --selected button, get item data
	local itemdata = game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:FindFirstChild(button.Name)
	local itemname = string.sub(button.Name,#category.Value + 2)
	gui.Desc.ItemName.Text = itemname
	gui.Desc.ItemDesc.Text = itemdata.ItemDesc.Value
	gui.Desc.ItemCostDiscounted.Text = ""
	gui.Desc.DiscountSlash.Visible = false
	gui.Desc.Savings.Text = ""
	gui.Desc.LimitedTimeNotice.Text = ""
	
	gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextStrokeTransparency = .7
	gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextTransparency = 0
	
	if not itemdata:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") then
		if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(itemdata.ItemID.Value) then
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "You own this item"
			gui.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = false
			gui.Desc.TwitterNotice.Text = ""
		else
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: $"..itemdata.ItemCost.Value
			gui.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = true
			gui.Desc.TwitterNotice.Text = ""
			if button:FindFirstChild("LocalOldCost") then
				gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: $"..button.LocalOldCost.Value
				gui.Desc.DiscountSlash.Visible = true
				gui.Desc.ItemCostDiscounted.Text = "$"..itemdata.ItemCost.Value
				gui.Desc.Savings.Text = "You save: $"..button.LocalOldCost.Value - itemdata.ItemCost.Value.."!"
				
				gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
				gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextTransparency = .5
			end
			if button:FindFirstChild("OffSaleTime") then
				local timeleft = button.OffSaleTime.Value - game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value
				local days = math.floor(timeleft / 86400)
				local timeleftstr = (days >= 2 and (days.." days")) or (days == 1 and "1 day") or "less than 1 day"
				gui.Desc.LimitedTimeNotice.Text = "Off-sale in: "..timeleftstr
			end
		end
	else
		if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(itemdata.ItemID.Value) then
			gui.Desc.TwitterNotice.Text = "You've unlocked this item from being an RHS Fan Club member!"
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = ""
			gui.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = false
		else
			gui.Desc.TwitterNotice.Text = "Join the RHS Fan Club to unlock this item! (Link in the game description)"
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = ""
			gui.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = false
		end
	end
	
	for i,v in pairs(gui.Items:GetChildren()) do
		v.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
	end
	button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,.25)
	gui.SelectedItem.Value = button.ItemID.Value
	setButtonColors()
end)

setButtonColors()

		failure.ProductId.Value = 0
		failure.Reason.Text = reason

		local highestprod

				local diff = tonumber(v.Name) - reason
				if diff >= 0 and (bestprod == nil or tonumber(bestprod.Name) > tonumber(v.Name)) then
					bestprod = v
				end
				if highestprod == nil or tonumber(v.Name) > tonumber(highestprod.Name) then
					highestprod = v
				end
			end
		end
		if bestprod == nil then
			bestprod = highestprod
		end

		failure.Reason.Text = "You don't have enough Cash to buy this item. Would you like to get more Cash?"

		failure.ProductId.Value = bestprod.Value
		failure.BuyCashButtons.GetCash.Text = "Get +"..bestprod.Name.." Cash!"
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = false
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = true
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	end
end

function getAvailableItemCount(id)
	local count = 0
	for _,v in pairs(data.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if tonumber(v.Name) == id then
			count = count + 1
		end
	end
	local furnbought = data.FurnitureCost:FindFirstChild(id)
	if furnbought then
		count = count + furnbought.PendingSales.Value + furnbought.Value --items in Objects - sold items - added items (or + removed items)
	end
	return count
end

		savedcategory.Value = categoryname

			end
		end

			if v.Category.Value == selectedcategory.Value and not (v:FindFirstChild("NotForSale") and getAvailableItemCount(v.ItemID.Value) <= 0) and not (v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and v.ReleaseTime.Value > game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value) then

				button.Parent = gui.Items
				button.Name = v.Name
				button.ItemID.Value = v.ItemID.Value
				button.Image = "http://www.roblox.com/Thumbs/Asset.ashx?width=75&height=75&assetId=".. v.ModelID.Value
				button.Position = UDim2.new(0, xv*75, 0, yv*75)
				if v:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") then
					local bird = script.TwitterBird:clone()
					bird.Parent = button
					bird.Visible = true
				end
				if v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*21 then
					local newb = script.NewBanner:clone()
					newb.Parent = button
					newb.Visible = true
				end
				xv = xv + 1
				if xv == 4 then
					xv = 0
					yv = yv + 1
				end
				button.Visible = true
			end
		end
		
		gui.Items.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,(yv + 1) * 75)
		gui.LoadItemInfo:Fire()
		setButtonColors()

			if v.Name == categoryname then
				v.BackgroundTransparency = 0
			else
				v.BackgroundTransparency = .5
			end
		end
	end
end

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local mp = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")

local gui = script.Parent:WaitForChild("Frame")
local shirtid = gui:WaitForChild("ShirtID")
local pantsid = gui:WaitForChild("PantsID")

local module = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.InterfaceModule)
module.openWindow(script.Parent.Frame)

wait(.09)

--print(game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):GetProductInfo(581285249).Creator.CreatorTargetId)

function getClothingId(valueobj)
	if valueobj.Value ~= 0 then
		local asset
		local origid = valueobj.Value
		pcall(function()
			asset = mp:GetProductInfo(origid)
		end)
		if asset then
			local creator = asset.Creator.CreatorTargetId
			local count = 0
			local success
			local clothingasset
			local groups
			repeat
				pcall(function() clothingasset = mp:GetProductInfo(origid+(count+1)) end)
				if clothingasset and (clothingasset.AssetTypeId == 11 or clothingasset.AssetTypeId == 12) then
					if clothingasset.Creator.CreatorTargetId == creator then
						--surefire hit
						success = true
					elseif clothingasset.Creator.CreatorType == "Group" then
						if groups == nil then
							pcall(function()
								groups = game:GetService("GroupService"):GetGroupsAsync(creator)
							end)
						end
						if groups then
							for _,entry in pairs(groups) do
								if entry["Id"] == clothingasset.Creator.CreatorTargetId then
									success = true
									break
								end
							end
						end
					else
						clothingasset = nil
					end
				else
					clothingasset = nil
				end
				count = count + 1
			until success == true or count > 25
			if success == true then
				valueobj.Value = origid + count
			else
				print("failed to load clothing asset")
			end
		end
	end
end

getClothingId(shirtid)
getClothingId(pantsid)

--http://www.roblox.com/Thumbs/Asset.ashx?width=110&height=110&assetId=187347200

if shirtid.Value ~= 0 and pantsid.Value ~= 0 then --both
	gui.TwoItemsContainer.Shirt.ImageButton.Image = "http://www.roblox.com/Thumbs/Asset.ashx?width=110&height=110&assetId="..shirtid.Value
	gui.TwoItemsContainer.Pants.ImageButton.Image = "http://www.roblox.com/Thumbs/Asset.ashx?width=110&height=110&assetId="..pantsid.Value
	gui.TwoItemsContainer.Visible = true
else
	for _,v in pairs(gui:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name:match("ID") and v:IsA("IntValue") and v.Value ~= 0 then
			local container = gui.OneItem
			container.Name = string.sub(v.Name,1,5)
			container.ImageButton.Image = "http://www.roblox.com/Thumbs/Asset.ashx?width=110&height=110&assetId="..v.Value
			container.Visible = true
		end
	end
end

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local stats = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(plr.userId):WaitForChild("LoyaltyStats")
local rs = game:GetService("RunService")
local frame = script.Parent:WaitForChild("Frame")

--empty, yellow, purple
local colors = {Color3.new(74/255,58/255,0), Color3.new(1,234/255,0), Color3.new(1,44/255,220/255)}

function commanumber(num)
	local num = tostring(num)
	local i = 0
	local finalstring = ""
	for n in string.gmatch(num,"%d") do
		finalstring = finalstring..n
		i = i + 1
		if (#num - i) % 3 == 0 and i ~= #num then
			finalstring = finalstring..","
		end
	end
	return finalstring
end

function SetBarOverheadText()
	frame:WaitForChild("ConsecDaysCount").Text = stats.Spins.Value == 0 and "Visit 5 days in a row = free RHS prize spins!" or stats.Spins.Value > 0 and "Congrats! You earned "..stats.Spins.Value.." free prize spin"..(stats.Spins.Value == 1 and "" or "s").."!"
end

--set up the text based on data
frame:WaitForChild("LPReward").Text = "+"..commanumber(stats.LPReward.Value).." LP"
frame:WaitForChild("LPDayBonus").Text = "(500+"..(stats.ConsecDays.Value > 5 and 5 or stats.ConsecDays.Value) * 100 ..": "..stats.ConsecDays.Value.." Day Bonus)"
local pre_lp = stats.LP.Value - stats.LPReward.Value
frame:WaitForChild("TotalLP").Text = commanumber(pre_lp)
SetBarOverheadText()

local startingbars = stats.ConsecDays.Value - 1
if startingbars >= 5 then
	for i=1,5 do
		frame:WaitForChild("ConsecDaysBar"):WaitForChild(i).BackgroundColor3 = colors[2]
	end
end
for i=1, (startingbars % 5) do
	frame:WaitForChild("ConsecDaysBar"):WaitForChild(i).BackgroundColor3 = startingbars > 5 and colors[3] or colors[2]
end

local module = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.InterfaceModule)
module.openWindow(script.Parent.Frame,true)

function TextTransparencyFade(element, totalframes, fadein)
	coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
		local i = 0
		local transincrement = (1 - element.TextTransparency) / totalframes
		local strokeincrement = (1 - element.TextStrokeTransparency) / totalframes
		local mult = 1
		if fadein then
			mult = -1
			element.TextTransparency = 1
			element.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
		end
		element.Visible = true
		local render = rs.RenderStepped:connect(function()
			if i < totalframes then
				element.TextTransparency = element.TextTransparency + transincrement * mult
				element.TextStrokeTransparency = element.TextStrokeTransparency + strokeincrement * mult
				i = i + 1
			end
		end)
		repeat wait() until i >= totalframes
		if fadein == false then
			element.Visible = false
			element.TextTransparency = 1 - (transincrement * totalframes)
			element.TextStrokeTransparency = 1 - (strokeincrement * totalframes)
		end
		render:disconnect()
	end),element, totalframes, fadein)
end

function ImageFade(element, totalframes, fadein)
	coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
		local i = 0
		local increment = 1 / totalframes
		local mult = 1
		if fadein then
			mult = -1
			element.ImageTransparency = 1
		else
			element.ImageTransparency = 0
		end
		element.Visible = true
		local render = rs.RenderStepped:connect(function()
			if i < totalframes then
				element.ImageTransparency = element.ImageTransparency + increment * mult
				i = i + 1
			end
		end)
		repeat wait() until i >= totalframes
		if fadein == false then
			element.Visible = false
		end
		render:disconnect()
	end),element, totalframes, fadein)
end

function PlaySound(id,timelength,volume,pitch)
	local sound = Instance.new("Sound",script.Parent)
	sound.Name = "LoyaltySound"
	sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..id
	sound.Volume = volume or 0.5
	sound.Pitch = pitch or 1
	sound:Play()
	game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,timelength or 3)
end

--handle spins if there are any - use a bindableevent to detect button clicks

--base window size: 0, 360,0, 160
--base window position: 0.5, -180,0.5, -110

--showing first "Spin for Prizes" button: 
--expand window size 0, 360,0, 210
--position: 0.5, -180,0.5, -110 (same)

--expanding for spinner:
--size: 0, 360,0, 270
--position: 0.5, -180,0.5, -130

--spinner colors:
--common: 112, 255, 112
--uncommon: 27, 235, 27
--rare: 255, 48, 7
--ultra rare: 255, 226, 0


--[[format of spin prize table:
	{  {"ItemName" = "Generic Item Name", "ItemID" = 210, "ImageID" = "rbxassetid://12345678", "PrizeTier" = 1, "IsCash" = false}, [...]  }
	if IsCash, then ItemID is the amount
--]]

local spinnercolors = {Color3.new(122/255, 1, 122/255), Color3.new(20/255, 235/255, 20/255), Color3.new(1, 48/255, 7/255), Color3.new(1, 226/255, 0)}

if stats.Spins.Value > 0 then
	
	local prizetable
	
	function ShowSpinPrize(tbl, index, winner)
		local item = tbl[index]
		frame.SpinnerFrame.BackgroundColor3 = spinnercolors[item["PrizeTier"]]
		frame.SpinnerFrame.ItemName.Text = item["ItemName"]
		frame.SpinnerFrame.ItemPic.Image = item["ImageID"]
		PlaySound(203785584,.5,.30,2)
		if winner then --final prize
			PlaySound(131322514,2,.5,1.5)
			if item["PrizeTier"] == 4 then
				PlaySound(345563471,10)
			else
				PlaySound(140413818)
			end
			if item["IsCash"] == true then
				PlaySound(131886985)
			end
			--play star animations?
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PrizeWheelStopped:FireServer()
			SetBarOverheadText()
			frame.SpinnerFrame.Header.Visible = true
			frame.ShopButton.Visible = true
			frame.CloseButton.Visible = true
		else
			frame.SpinnerFrame.Header.Visible = false
		end
	end
	
	frame:TweenSizeAndPosition(UDim2.new(0,360,0,210), UDim2.new(0.5, -180,0.5, -110),...,..., .25,true)
	wait(.25)
	frame.OpenSpinner.Visible = true
	frame.OpenSpinner.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
		TextTransparencyFade(frame.RHSLPLabel, 20, false)
		TextTransparencyFade(frame.TotalLP, 20, false)
		frame.OpenSpinner.Visible = false
		PlaySound(345593426,nil,.8)
		frame:TweenSizeAndPosition(UDim2.new(0,360,0,240), UDim2.new(0.5, -180,0.5, -125),nil,nil, .4,true)
		wait(.32)
		
		local spinning = false
		local cyclerate = 12
		local winningindex
		
		local function CycleSpinner()
			local recentlyusedtable = {}
			local function DupeCheck(i)
				for _,v in pairs(recentlyusedtable) do
					if v == i then
						return true
					end
				end
				return false
			end
			while spinning do
				local indextodisplay
				local winner = false
				if winningindex ~= nil and DupeCheck(winningindex) == false then
					indextodisplay = winningindex
					winner = true
				else
					repeat
						local n = math.random(1,#prizetable)
						if DupeCheck(n) == false then
							indextodisplay = n
						end
					until indextodisplay ~= nil
				end
				table.insert(recentlyusedtable,indextodisplay)
				if #recentlyusedtable > 5 then
					table.remove(recentlyusedtable,1)
				end
				
				ShowSpinPrize(prizetable,indextodisplay,winner)
				
				if winner then
					spinning = false
					if stats.Spins.Value > 0 then
						frame.RespinButton.Visible = true
					end
					return
				end
				
				wait(1/cyclerate)
			end
		end
		
		local function RunSpin()
			winningindex = nil
			frame.RespinButton.Visible = false
			frame.ShopButton.Visible = false
			frame.CloseButton.Visible = false
			cyclerate = 12
			prizetable = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.FetchSpinPrizeTable:InvokeServer()
			frame.SpinnerFrame.Visible = true
			frame.StopSpinner.Visible = true
			spinning = true
			spawn(CycleSpinner)
			
			local stopcon
			stopcon = frame.StopSpinner.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
				stopcon:disconnect()
				frame.StopSpinner.Visible = false
				PlaySound(345593426,nil,.8)
				wait(math.random(3,6)/10)
				for i=1,10 do
					wait(.6 + math.random(1,10)/100 - cyclerate / 40)
					cyclerate = cyclerate - 1
				end
				wait(math.random(1,25)/10)
				winningindex = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.FetchWinningSpinPrize:InvokeServer()
				local respincon
				respincon = frame.RespinButton.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
					if stats.Spins.Value > 0 and spinning == false then
						respincon:disconnect()
						PlaySound(345593426,nil,.8)
						RunSpin()
					end
				end)
			end)
		end
		
		RunSpin()
	end)
end

function commanumber(num)
	local num = tostring(num)
	local i = 0
	local finalstring = ""
	for n in string.gmatch(num,"%d") do
		finalstring = finalstring..n
		i = i + 1
		if (#num - i) % 3 == 0 and i ~= #num then
			finalstring = finalstring..","
		end
	end
	return finalstring
end

function displayFailureWindow(result,reason) --reason == amount of cash needed
	if result == false then
		
		local failure = shopframe.FailureWindow
		failure.ProductId.Value = 0
		failure.Reason.Text = reason
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = true
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = false
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	elseif result == "cash" then
		
		local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
		local cash = data.cash.Value
		local products = game.ReplicatedStorage.CashProducts:GetChildren()
		local bestprod
		local highestprod
		for i,v in pairs(products) do
			local diff = tonumber(v.Name) - reason
			if diff >= 0 and (bestprod == nil or tonumber(bestprod.Name) > tonumber(v.Name)) then
				bestprod = v
			end
			if highestprod == nil or tonumber(v.Name) > tonumber(highestprod.Name) then
				highestprod = v
			end
		end
		if bestprod == nil then
			bestprod = highestprod
		end
		
		local failure = shopframe.FailureWindow
		failure.Reason.Text = "You don't have enough Cash to buy this item. Would you like to get more Cash?"
		failure.CashAmount.Value = tonumber(bestprod.Name)
		failure.ProductId.Value = bestprod.Value
		failure.BuyCashButtons.GetCash.Text = "Get +"..bestprod.Name.." Cash!"
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = false
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = true
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	end
end


function setButtonColors()
	for _,button in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
		if button.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
			if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(button.ItemID.Value) then --has object
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.5,1,.5)
				button.ItemCost.Text = ""
			else
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
			end
			if button.ItemID.Value == 288 then
				button.ItemCost.Text = ""
			end
			if plr.SelectedCar.Value == button.ItemID.Value then
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,.25)
			end
		end
	end
end

function setNavigationButtonColors()
	local canvaswidth = frame.CanvasSize.X.Offset
	local canvaspos = frame.CanvasPosition.X
	local framewidth = frame.AbsoluteSize.X
	
	local isat
	
	if canvaspos == 0 then
		isat = 0
	elseif canvaspos >= canvaswidth - framewidth then
		isat = 1
	end
	
	if isat == 0 then
		backbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(107, 120, 245)
		backbutton.ImageTransparency = .7
	else
		backbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(128, 244, 120)
		backbutton.ImageTransparency = 0
	end
	
	if isat == 1 then
		forwardbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(107, 120, 245)
		forwardbutton.ImageTransparency = .7
	else
		forwardbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(128, 244, 120)
		forwardbutton.ImageTransparency = 0
	end
end

	shopframe:WaitForChild("BuyButton").Visible = false

			v:Destroy()
		end
	end

	local objectstable = {}
	
	local objdata = game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects
	for _,v in pairs(objdata:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name:match("vehic_") and not (v:FindFirstChild("OldSchoolCar")) and not (v:FindFirstChild("NotForSale") and data.Objects:FindFirstChild(v.ItemID.Value) == nil) and not ((v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v.ReleaseTime.Value < 0) and game.PlaceId == 9689581) and not (v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") and v.TimeToGoOffSale.Value < game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value and data.Objects:FindFirstChild(v.ItemID.Value) == nil) then
			table.insert(objectstable,v)
		end
	end
	
	local function sortfunc(v1, v2)
		if (v1:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v1.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24) or (v2:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v2.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24) then
			if v1:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v1.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24 and (v2:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") == nil or game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v2.ReleaseTime.Value > 86400*24) then
				return true
			elseif v2:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v2.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24 and (v1:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") == nil or game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v1.ReleaseTime.Value > 86400*24) then
				return false
			elseif v1:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v1.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24 and v2:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v2.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24 then
				if v1.ReleaseTime.Value > v2.ReleaseTime.Value then
					return true
				elseif v1.ReleaseTime.Value < v2.ReleaseTime.Value then
					return false
				else
					if v1.ItemID.Value > v2.ItemID.Value then
						return true
					else
						return false
					end
				end
			end
		end
		
		if v1.ItemCost.Value < v2.ItemCost.Value then
			return true
		else
			return false
		end
	end
	
	table.sort(objectstable,sortfunc)

					end
				end
			end
			button.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
		end
		if v:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") then
			local icon = script.TwitterBird:clone()
			icon.Parent = button
			icon.Visible = true
			for _,x in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
				if x.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
					if x ~= button then
						x.Position = x.Position + UDim2.new(0,100,0,0)
					end
				end
			end
			button.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
		end
			
		button.Visible = true
	end
	frame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,(#frame:GetChildren() - 1) * 100,0,0)

	local item = getItemDataFromID(id)
	if item then
		if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(id) then
			shopframe.BuyButton.Text = "Spawn"
		else
			if not item:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") then
				shopframe.BuyButton.Text = "Buy Car"
			else
				shopframe.BuyButton.Visible = false
			end
		end
		if item:FindFirstChild("OffSaleTime") then
			local timeleft = item.OffSaleTime.Value - game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value

function getItemDataFromID(id)
	for i,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if v.ItemID.Value == id then
			return v
		end
	end

function displayFailureWindow(result,reason) --reason == amount of cash needed
	if result == false then
		
		local failure = shopframe.FailureWindow
		failure.ProductId.Value = 0
		failure.Reason.Text = reason
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = true
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = false
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	elseif result == "cash" then
		
		local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
		local cash = data.cash.Value
		local products = game.ReplicatedStorage.CashProducts:GetChildren()
		local bestprod
		local highestprod
		for i,v in pairs(products) do
			local diff = tonumber(v.Name) - reason
			if diff >= 0 and (bestprod == nil or tonumber(bestprod.Name) > tonumber(v.Name)) then
				bestprod = v
			end
			if highestprod == nil or tonumber(v.Name) > tonumber(highestprod.Name) then
				highestprod = v
			end
		end
		if bestprod == nil then
			bestprod = highestprod
		end
		
		local failure = shopframe.FailureWindow
		failure.Reason.Text = "You don't have enough Cash to buy this item. Would you like to get more Cash?"
		failure.CashAmount.Value = tonumber(bestprod.Name)
		failure.ProductId.Value = bestprod.Value
		failure.BuyCashButtons.GetCash.Text = "Get +"..bestprod.Name.." Cash!"
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = false
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = true
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	end
end


function setButtonColors()
	for _,button in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
		if button.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
			if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(button.ItemID.Value) then --has object
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.5,1,.5)
				button.ItemCost.Text = ""
			else
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
			end
			if plr.SelectedPaint.Value == button.ItemID.Value then
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,.25)
			end
		end
	end
end

function setNavigationButtonColors()
	local canvaswidth = frame.CanvasSize.X.Offset
	local canvaspos = frame.CanvasPosition.X
	local framewidth = frame.AbsoluteSize.X
	
	local isat
	
	if canvaspos == 0 then
		isat = 0
	elseif canvaspos >= canvaswidth - framewidth then
		isat = 1
	end
	
	if isat == 0 then
		backbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(107, 120, 245)
		backbutton.ImageTransparency = .7
	else
		backbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(128, 244, 120)
		backbutton.ImageTransparency = 0
	end
	
	if isat == 1 then
		forwardbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(107, 120, 245)
		forwardbutton.ImageTransparency = .7
	else
		forwardbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(128, 244, 120)
		forwardbutton.ImageTransparency = 0
	end
end

local jointime = data:WaitForChild("FirstLoginTime").Value
local currenttimeobj = game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime
local timelabel = frame:WaitForChild("LimitedTimeNotice")

function GetTimeString(timeleft)
	if timeleft < 0 then
		return "Ends now!"
	end
	
	local d, h, m, s 
	
	d = math.floor(timeleft / 86400)
	if d == 0 then
		d = ""
	else
		d = d..":"
	end
	
	h = math.floor((timeleft / 3600) % 24)
	if h < 10 then
		if h == 0 then
			if d == "" then
				h = ""
			else
				h = "00:"
			end
		else
			if d == "" then
				h = h..":"
			else
				h = "0"..h..":"
			end
		end
	else
		h = h..":"
	end
	
	m = math.floor((timeleft / 60) % 60)
	if m < 10 then
		if m == 0 then
			if h == "" then
				m = "0:"
			else
				m = "00:"
			end
		else
			if h == "" then
				m = m..":"
			else
				m = "0"..m..":"
			end
		end
	else
		m = m..":"
	end
	
	s = timeleft % 60
	if s < 10 then
		s = "0"..s
	end
	
	return d..h..m..s
end

function SetTimeString()
	local timeleft = (jointime + 86400 * 7) - currenttimeobj.Value
	if timeleft <= 0 then
		timelabel.Text = "Offer about to expire!"
	else
		timelabel.Text = "Offer ends in "..GetTimeString(timeleft)
	end
end

SetTimeString()

currenttimeobj.Changed:connect(function()
	SetTimeString()
end)

--Made by Luckymaxer

Board = script.Parent
Model = Board.Parent

Players = game:GetService("Players")
Debris = game:GetService("Debris")
ContentProvider = game:GetService("ContentProvider")
UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

DisplayColor = Board:WaitForChild("DisplayColor").Value

SpeedBoostGui = script:WaitForChild("SpeedBoostGui")
LightSpeedEffectGui = script:WaitForChild("LightSpeedEffectGui")

ActiveAnimations = {}

RemoteControl = Board:WaitForChild("RemoteControl")
Animations = Board:WaitForChild("Animations")

ServerControl = RemoteControl:WaitForChild("ServerControl")
ClientControl = RemoteControl:WaitForChild("ClientControl")

Animations = {
	BoardKick = Animations:WaitForChild("BoardKick"),
	CoastingPose = Animations:WaitForChild("CoastingPose"),
	LeftTurn = Animations:WaitForChild("LeftTurn"),
	RightTurn = Animations:WaitForChild("RightTurn"),
	Ollie = Animations:WaitForChild("Ollie")
}

Sounds = {
	BoardDrop = Board:WaitForChild("BoardDrop"),
	BoardOllie = Board:WaitForChild("BoardOllie"),
	BoardStop = Board:WaitForChild("BoardStop"),
	CruiseLoop = Board:WaitForChild("CruiseLoop")
}

TopSpeed = 120
WaitTime = 0.1

EqualizingForce = (236 / 1.2) -- amount of force required to levitate 
Gravity = 0.9999999999999999 -- things float at > 1

CurrentSteer = 0
CurrentThrottle = 0

-- Velocity --
MaxSpeed = 80
BaseMaxSpeed = MaxSpeed
Acceleration = 20
Deceleration = 20

TurnAlpha = 1

AlphaDampening = 0.5

TurnSpeed = 0

--ClientControl.OnClientInvoke = (function(player, Mode, Value)
--end)

function InvokeServer(Mode, Value)
	local ServerReturn = nil
	pcall(function()
		ServerReturn = ServerControl:InvokeServer(Mode, Value)
	end)
	return ServerReturn
end

function SetAnimation(Mode, Value)
	if Mode == "Play" and Value and SkateboardEquipped and CheckIfAlive() then
		for i, v in pairs(ActiveAnimations) do
			if v.Animation == Value.Animation then
				v.AnimationTrack:Stop()
				table.remove(ActiveAnimations, i)
			end
		end
		local AnimationTrack = Humanoid:LoadAnimation(Value.Animation)
		table.insert(ActiveAnimations, {Animation = Value.Animation, AnimationTrack = AnimationTrack})
		AnimationTrack:Play(Value.FadeTime, Value.Weight, Value.Speed)
	elseif Mode == "Stop" and Value then
		for i, v in pairs(ActiveAnimations) do
			if v.Animation == Value.Animation then
				v.AnimationTrack:Stop(Value.FadeTime)
				table.remove(ActiveAnimations, i)
			end
		end
	end
end

function UpdateGui()
	while ReloadPercent < 100 do
		wait(ReloadTime / 100)
		Gui.Bar.Fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,0,0)
		ReloadPercent = math.min(100, (ReloadPercent + 1))
		Gui.Bar.NameLabel.Text = "Speed Boost"
		Gui.Bar.PercentLabel.Text = (tostring(ReloadPercent) .. "%")
		Gui.Bar.Fill.Size = UDim2.new((-1 * ReloadPercent / 100), 0, 0, 6)
	end
	Gui.Bar.Fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
	Gui.Bar.NameLabel.Text = "Speed Boost"
	Gui.Bar.PercentLabel.Text = "100%"
	Gui.Bar.Fill.Size = UDim2.new(-1, 0, 0, 6)
end

function DrainGui()
	while ReloadPercent > 0 do
		wait(Duration / 100)
		Gui.Bar.Fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
		ReloadPercent = math.max(0, (ReloadPercent - 1))
		Gui.Bar.NameLabel.Text = "Speed Boost"
		Gui.Bar.PercentLabel.Text = (tostring(ReloadPercent) .. "%")
		Gui.Bar.Fill.Size = UDim2.new((-1 * ReloadPercent / 100), 0, 0, 6)
	end
end

function ShowBoostGui()
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	
	local EffectGui = LightSpeedEffectGui:Clone()
	local Whirl = EffectGui.Whirl
	local Background = Whirl.Background
	local Text = Whirl.TextLabel
	
	for i, v in pairs({Whirl, Background, Text}) do
		if v:IsA("TextLabel") then
			v.TextColor3 = DisplayColor
		elseif v:IsA("ImageLabel") then
			v.ImageColor3 = DisplayColor
		end
	end
	
	Debris:AddItem(EffectGui, 5)
	EffectGui.Parent = PlayerGui	
	
	if PlayerGui and PlayerGui.Parent then
		EffectGui.Parent = PlayerGui
	end
	
	for i = 1, 2.5, 0.02 do
		Text.TextTransparency = (Text.TextTransparency + 0.05)
		Text.TextStrokeTransparency = Text.TextTransparency
		Whirl.Size = UDim2.new(i, 1, i, 1)
		Whirl.Position = UDim2.new((0.5 - i / 2), 0, (0.5 - i / 2), 0)
		wait(0.04)
	end
	
	if EffectGui and EffectGui.Parent then
		EffectGui:Destroy()
	end
end

function GetAllConnectedParts(Object)
	local Parts = {}
	local function GetConnectedParts(Object)
		for i, v in pairs(Object:GetConnectedParts()) do
			local Ignore = false
			for ii, vv in pairs(Parts) do
				if v == vv then
					Ignore = true
				end
			end
			if not Ignore then
				table.insert(Parts, v)
				GetConnectedParts(v)
			end
		end
	end
	GetConnectedParts(Object)
	return Parts
end

function ThrustUpdater()
	while SkateboardEquipped do
		RotationForce.angularvelocity = Vector3.new(0, TurnSpeed, 0)
		
		local Direction = Board.CFrame.lookVector
		Direction = Vector3.new(Direction.X, 0, Direction.Z).unit
		ThrustForce.velocity = (Direction * CurrentSpeed)

		if math.abs(TurnSpeed) > AlphaDampening then
			TurnSpeed = (TurnSpeed - (AlphaDampening * (math.abs(TurnSpeed) / TurnSpeed)))
		else 
			TurnSpeed = 0
		end
		local LeanAmount = (-TurnSpeed * (math.pi / 6) / 0.8)
		TurnGyro.cframe = CFrame.Angles((LeanAmount * Direction.X), 0, (LeanAmount * Direction.Z))
		
		--Compare CurrentSpeed (speed supposed to be traveling at) to actual velocity.
		local BoardVelocity = Vector3.new(Board.Velocity.X, 0, Board.Velocity.Z).magnitude
		if CurrentSpeed > 5 and (BoardVelocity + 6) < CurrentSpeed then
			CurrentSpeed = math.max((CurrentSpeed - 7),0)
		end
		
		wait(WaitTime)
	end
end

function BoostSpeed(Speed)
	EnterPushingState()
	MaxSpeed = (MaxSpeed + Speed)
	CurrentSpeed = MaxSpeed
	Spawn(function()
		ShowBoostGui()
	end)
	DrainGui()
	MaxSpeed = BaseMaxSpeed
	UpdateGui()
	JumpEnabled = true
end


function DidAction(Action)
	LastAction = Action
	LastTime = time()
end

function AxisChanged(Axis)
	print("AxisChanged:",Axis)
	Board.Steer = Controller.Steer
	Board.Throttle = Controller.Throttle
	
	CurrentSteer = Board.Steer
	
	if CurrentSteer == -1 then --Left turn
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.Ollie})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.RightTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.LeftTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		DidAction("Left")
		while Board.Steer == -1 do
			TurnSpeed = math.min(TurnMax, (TurnSpeed + TurnAlpha))
			wait(WaitTime)
		end
	elseif CurrentSteer == 1 then --Right turn
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.LeftTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.Ollie})
		SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.RightTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		DidAction("Right")
		while Board.Steer == 1 do
			TurnSpeed = math.max((-1 * TurnMax), (TurnSpeed - TurnAlpha))
			wait(WaitTime)
		end
	elseif CurrentSteer == 0 then --Forward/Backward
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.LeftTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.Ollie})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.RightTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		if (LastAction == "Go" and time() - LastTime < 0.1) then
		end
		DidAction("Go")
		if not Sounds.CruiseLoop.IsPlaying then
			Sounds.CruiseLoop:Play()
		end
	end
end

function ThrottleChanged(axis)
	print("ThrottleChanged:",axis)
	if Controller.Throttle ~= CurrentThrottle then
		CurrentThrottle = Controller.Throttle
		if CurrentThrottle > 0 then
			while CurrentThrottle > 0 do
				CurrentSpeed = math.min(MaxSpeed, (CurrentSpeed + (Acceleration * (WaitTime))))
				wait(WaitTime)
			end
		end
		if CurrentThrottle < 0 then
			while CurrentThrottle < 0 do
				CurrentSpeed = math.max((-1 * MaxSpeed), (CurrentSpeed - (Deceleration * (WaitTime))))
				wait(WaitTime)
			end
		end
	end
end

function EnterAirState(Boolean)
	if Boolean then
		JumpEnabled = false
		Sounds.CruiseLoop:Stop()
		Sounds.BoardOllie:Play()
	else
		JumpEnabled = true
		Sounds.BoardDrop:Play()
		Sounds.CruiseLoop:Stop()
	end
end

function EnterPushingState()
	SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
end

function EnterCoastingState()
	if not Sounds.CruiseLoop.IsPlaying then
		Sounds.CruiseLoop:Play()
	end
end

function EnterStoppingState()
	Sounds.CruiseLoop:Stop()
	BreakModifyAndPlay()
end

function MoveStateChanged(NewState, OldState)
	if not JumpEnabled then
		EnterAirState(false)
	end
	if NewState == Enum.MoveState.AirFree then
		EnterAirState(true)
	elseif NewState == Enum.MoveState.Pushing then
--		EnterPushingState()
	elseif NewState == Enum.MoveState.Coasting then
		EnterCoastingState()
	elseif NewState == Enum.MoveState.Stopping then
		EnterStoppingState()
	end
end

function SetUpControl()
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	RotationForce = Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity")
	RotationForce.maxTorque = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
	RotationForce.angularvelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	RotationForce.Parent = Torso

	ThrustForce = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
	ThrustForce.maxForce = Vector3.new(30000, 0, 30000)
	ThrustForce.maxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, 0, math.huge)
	ThrustForce.velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	ThrustForce.P = 800
	ThrustForce.Parent = Board
	
	TurnGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
	TurnGyro.maxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, 0, math.huge)
	TurnGyro.Parent = Torso
	
	local Mass = 0
	for i, v in pairs(GetAllConnectedParts(Board)) do
		Mass = (Mass + v:GetMass() * EqualizingForce * Gravity)
	end
	
	AntiGrav = Instance.new("BodyForce")
	AntiGrav.force = Vector3.new(0, (Mass / 2), 0)
	AntiGrav.Parent = Board
	
end

function ButtonChanged(Button)
	local ButtonState = Controller:GetButton(Button)
	if not ButtonState then
		return
	end
	if Button == Enum.Button.Dismount then
		Board.ControllingHumanoid.Jump = ButtonState
	elseif Button == Enum.Button.Jump then
		if JumpEnabled and ReloadPercent == 100 then
			JumpEnabled = false
			BoostSpeed(20)
		end
	end
end

function CruiseModify()
	local Velocity = Board.Velocity.magnitude
	local LowPitch = 0.85
	local TopPitch = 1.1
	local LowV = 0.1
	local TopV = 1
	if Velocity < 1 then
		Sounds.CruiseLoop.Volume = 0
		return
	end
	Sounds.CruiseLoop.Volume = math.min((((TopV - LowV) * (Velocity / TopSpeed)) + LowV), 1)
	Sounds.CruiseLoop.Pitch = (((TopPitch - LowPitch) * (Velocity / MaxSpeed)) + LowPitch)
end

function BreakModifyAndPlay()
	local v = Board.Velocity.magnitude
	local Slowest = 10
	local LowV = 0.05
	local TopV = 0.8
	if (v < Slowest) then
		return
	end
	Sounds.BoardStop.Volume = math.min((((TopV - LowV) * (v / (MaxSpeed - Slowest))) + LowV), 1)
	Sounds.BoardStop:Play()
end

function CheckIfAlive()
	return (((Player and Player.Parent and Humanoid and Humanoid.Parent and Humanoid.Health > 0 and Head and Head.Parent and Torso and Torso.Parent) and true) or false)
end

	Controller = controller

	Torso = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	Player = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Character)
	PlayerGui = Player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	Camera = game:GetService("Workspace").CurrentCamera
	Camera.CameraSubject = Head
	Camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Follow

		if v then
			v:disconnect()
		end
	end
	for i, v in pairs({Animations, Sounds}) do
		for ii, vv in pairs(v) do
			if vv:IsA("Animation") then
				ContentProvider:Preload(vv.AnimationId)
			elseif vv:IsA("Sound") then
				ContentProvider:Preload(vv.SoundId)
			end
		end
	end
	KeyDownConnection = UserInputService.InputBegan:connect(function(InputObject)
		if InputObject.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
			InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Down = true, Key = InputObject.KeyCode})
			print("true",InputObject.KeyCode)
		end
	end)
	KeyUpConnection = UserInputService.InputEnded:connect(function(InputObject)
		if InputObject.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
			InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Down = false, Key = InputObject.KeyCode})
			print("false",InputObject.KeyCode)
		end
	end)
	
	MobileControls = nil	
	
	local Emulation = Player:FindFirstChild("KeyboardEmulation")
	if Emulation then
		
		MobileControls = coroutine.create(function()
			while MobileControls ~= nil do
				if Emulation.w.Value == true then
					Board.Throttle = 1
					CurrentSpeed = math.min(MaxSpeed, (CurrentSpeed + (Acceleration * (0.03))))
				elseif Emulation.s.Value == true then
					Board.Throttle = -1
					CurrentSpeed = math.max((-1 * MaxSpeed), (CurrentSpeed - (Deceleration * (0.03))))
				else
					Board.Throttle = 0
				end
				
				if Emulation.a.Value == true then
					Board.Steer = -1
					TurnSpeed = math.min(TurnMax, (TurnSpeed + TurnAlpha))
				elseif Emulation.d.Value == true then
					Board.Steer = 1
					TurnSpeed = math.max((-1 * TurnMax), (TurnSpeed - TurnAlpha))
				else
					Board.Steer = 0
				end
				wait()
			end
		end)
		coroutine.resume(MobileControls)
		
		for _,v in pairs(Emulation:GetChildren()) do
			v.Changed:connect(function(val)
				local enum
				if v.Name == " " then
					enum = Enum.KeyCode.Space
				elseif v.Name ~= "MouseIsDown" then
					enum = Enum.KeyCode[string.upper(v.Name)]
				end
				if enum then
					InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Down = val, Key = enum})
					if enum == Enum.KeyCode.Space and val == true then
						ButtonChanged(Enum.Button.Jump,true)
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
	for i, v in  pairs(Board:GetChildren()) do
		if v:IsA("BodyForce") or v:IsA("BodyGyro") or v:IsA("BodyVelocity") or v:IsA("BodyAngularVelocity") then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	SkateboardEquipped = true
	Board.Friction = 0
	Board.StickyWheels = true
	SetUpControl()
	Spawn(function()
		if PlayerGui then
			Gui = SpeedBoostGui:Clone()
			UpdateGui()
			Gui.Parent = PlayerGui
		end
	end)
	Spawn(ThrustUpdater)
	Controller.AxisChanged:connect(AxisChanged)
	if not MobileControls then
		Controller.AxisChanged:connect(ThrottleChanged)
	end
	Board.MoveStateChanged:connect(MoveStateChanged)
	Controller:BindButton(Enum.Button.Jump, "")
	Controller.ButtonChanged:connect(ButtonChanged)
	SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.CoastingPose})
	InvokeServer("Equipped", Character)
end

function Unequipped()
	Controller = nil
	Board.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	Board.RotVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	Board.Friction = 0.5
	Board.StickyWheels = false
	if CheckIfAlive() then
		Camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
		Camera.CameraSubject = Humanoid
		Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
	end
	for i, v in pairs({RotationForce, ThrustForce, TurnGyro, AntiGrav, Gui}) do
		if v and v.Parent then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	for i, v in pairs({KeyDownConnection, KeyUpConnection}) do
		if v then
			v:disconnect()
		end
	end
	MobileControls = nil
	for i, v in pairs(Animations) do
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = v})
	end
	for i, v in pairs(Sounds) do
		v:Stop()
	end
	ActiveAnimations = {}
	SkateboardEquipped = false
	InvokeServer("Unequipped")
end

Board.Equipped:connect(Equipped)
Board.Unequipped:connect(Unequipped)

RunService.Heartbeat:connect(function()
	CruiseModify()

		local plrgui=plr.PlayerGui
		if plrgui~=nil then
			gui.Parent=plrgui
		end

			local chr=sp.Parent
			if chr and check and mouse.Target~=nil and mouse.TargetSurface~=nil and mouse.Target.Name~="Spray" and mouse.Target.Name~="Effect" and sprayid>0 then
				local surface=mouse.TargetSurface
				local t=chr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local h=chr:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
				local animobject=sp:FindFirstChild("SprayPaint")
				if t and h and animobject then
					if h.Health>0 then
						check=false
						anim=h:LoadAnimation(animobject)
						if anim then
							anim:Play()
							sp.Handle.Sound:Play()
						end
						script.Parent.SprayEvent:FireServer(sprayid,t.Position, mouse.Hit.p, surface, mouse.Target)
						wait(cooldown)
						sp.Handle.Sound:Stop()
						check=true
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

	gui.Parent=sp
	equipped=false

function SetAnimation(mode, value)
	if mode == "PlayAnimation" and value and ToolEquipped and Humanoid then
		for i, v in pairs(Animations) do
			if v.Animation == value.Animation then
				v.AnimationTrack:Stop()
				table.remove(Animations, i)
			end
		end
		local AnimationTrack = Humanoid:LoadAnimation(value.Animation)
		table.insert(Animations, {Animation = value.Animation, AnimationTrack = AnimationTrack})
		AnimationTrack:Play(value.FadeTime, value.Weight, value.Speed)
	elseif mode == "StopAnimation" and value then
		for i, v in pairs(Animations) do
			if v.Animation == value.Animation then
				v.AnimationTrack:Stop(value.FadeTime)
				table.remove(Animations, i)
			end
		end
	end
end

function CheckIfAlive()
	return (((Character and Character.Parent and Humanoid and Humanoid.Parent and Humanoid.Health > 0 and Player and Player.Parent) and true) or false)
end

function Equipped(Mouse)
	Character = Tool.Parent
	Player = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Character)
	Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
	ToolEquipped = true
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	Spawn(function()
		PlayerMouse = Mouse
		Mouse.Button1Down:connect(function()
			InvokeServer("Button1Click", {Down = true})
		end)
		Mouse.Button1Up:connect(function()
			InvokeServer("Button1Click", {Down = false})
		end)
		Mouse.KeyDown:connect(function(Key)
			InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Key = Key, Down = true})
		end)
		Mouse.KeyUp:connect(function(Key)
			InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Key = Key, Down = false})
		end)
		local PlayerGui = Player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
		if PlayerGui then
			if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
				InputCheckClone = InputCheck:Clone()
				InputCheckClone.InputButton.InputBegan:connect(function()
					InvokeServer("Button1Click", {Down = true})
				end)
				InputCheckClone.InputButton.InputEnded:connect(function()
					InvokeServer("Button1Click", {Down = false})
				end)
				InputCheckClone.Parent = PlayerGui
			end
		end
	end)
end

function Unequipped()
	if InputCheckClone and InputCheckClone.Parent then
		InputCheckClone:Destroy()
	end
	for i, v in pairs(Animations) do
		if v and v.AnimationTrack then
			v.AnimationTrack:Stop()
		end
	end
	Animations = {}
	ToolEquipped = false
end

function InvokeServer(mode, value)
	pcall(function()
		local ServerReturn = ServerControl:InvokeServer(mode, value)
		return ServerReturn
	end)
end

function OnClientInvoke(mode, value)
	if mode == "PlayAnimation" and value and ToolEquipped and Humanoid then
		SetAnimation("PlayAnimation", value)
	elseif mode == "StopAnimation" and value then
		SetAnimation("StopAnimation", value)
	elseif mode == "PlaySound" and value then
		value:Play()
	elseif mode == "StopSound" and value then
		value:Stop()
	elseif mode == "MousePosition" then
		return ((PlayerMouse and {Position = PlayerMouse.Hit.p, Target = PlayerMouse.Target}) or nil)
	end
end

ClientControl.OnClientInvoke = OnClientInvoke
Tool.Equipped:connect(Equipped)
Tool.Unequipped:connect(Unequipped)

--[[
	// FileName: DPad
	// Version 1.0
	// Written by: jmargh
	// Description: Implements DPad controls for touch devices
--]]

local Players = game:GetService('Players')
local GuiService = game:GetService('GuiService')

local DPad = {}

local MasterControl = require(script.Parent)

--[[ Script Variables ]]--
while not Players.LocalPlayer do
	wait()
end
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local DPadFrame = nil
local TouchObject = nil
local OnInputEnded = nil		-- defined in Create()

--[[ Constants ]]--
local DPAD_SHEET = "rbxasset://textures/ui/DPadSheet.png"
local COMPASS_DIR = {
	Vector3.new(1, 0, 0),			-- E
	Vector3.new(1, 0, 1).unit,		-- SE
	Vector3.new(0, 0, 1),			-- S
	Vector3.new(-1, 0, 1).unit,		-- SW
	Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0),			-- W
	Vector3.new(-1, 0, -1).unit,	-- NW
	Vector3.new(0, 0, -1),			-- N
	Vector3.new(1, 0, -1).unit,		-- NE
}

--[[ lua Function Cache ]]--
local ATAN2 = math.atan2
local FLOOR = math.floor
local PI = math.pi

--[[ Local Functions ]]--
local function createArrowLabel(name, position, size, rectOffset, rectSize)
	local image = Instance.new('ImageLabel')
	image.Name = name
	image.Image = DPAD_SHEET
	image.ImageRectOffset = rectOffset
	image.ImageRectSize = rectSize
	image.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	image.Size = size
	image.Position = position
	image.Parent = DPadFrame
	
	return image
end

local function getCenterPosition()
	return Vector2.new(DPadFrame.AbsolutePosition.x + DPadFrame.AbsoluteSize.x/2, DPadFrame.AbsolutePosition.y + DPadFrame.AbsoluteSize.y/2)
end

--[[ Public API ]]--
function DPad:Enable()
	DPadFrame.Visible = true
end

function DPad:Disable()
	DPadFrame.Visible = false
	OnInputEnded()
end

function DPad:Create(parentFrame)
	if DPadFrame then
		DPadFrame:Destroy()
		DPadFrame = nil
	end
	
	local position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -230)
	DPadFrame = Instance.new('Frame')
	DPadFrame.Name = "DPadFrame"
	DPadFrame.Active = true
	DPadFrame.Visible = false
	DPadFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 192, 0, 192)
	DPadFrame.Position = position
	DPadFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	
	local smArrowSize = UDim2.new(0, 23, 0, 23)
	local lgArrowSize = UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 64)
	local smImgOffset = Vector2.new(46, 46)
	local lgImgOffset = Vector2.new(128, 128)
	
	local bBtn = createArrowLabel("BackButton", UDim2.new(0.5, -32, 1, -64), lgArrowSize, Vector2.new(0, 0), lgImgOffset)
	local fBtn = createArrowLabel("ForwardButton", UDim2.new(0.5, -32, 0, 0), lgArrowSize, Vector2.new(0, 258), lgImgOffset)
	local lBtn = createArrowLabel("LeftButton", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -32), lgArrowSize, Vector2.new(129, 129), lgImgOffset)
	local rBtn = createArrowLabel("RightButton", UDim2.new(1, -64, 0.5, -32), lgArrowSize, Vector2.new(0, 129), lgImgOffset)
	local jumpBtn = createArrowLabel("JumpButton", UDim2.new(0.5, -32, 0.5, -32), lgArrowSize, Vector2.new(129, 0), lgImgOffset)
	local flBtn = createArrowLabel("ForwardLeftButton", UDim2.new(0, 35, 0, 35), smArrowSize, Vector2.new(129, 258), smImgOffset)
	local frBtn = createArrowLabel("ForwardRightButton", UDim2.new(1, -55, 0, 35), smArrowSize, Vector2.new(176, 258), smImgOffset)
	flBtn.Visible = false
	frBtn.Visible = false
	
	-- input connections
	jumpBtn.InputBegan:connect(function(inputObject)
		MasterControl:DoJump()
	end)
	
	local movementVector = Vector3.new(0,0,0)
	local function normalizeDirection(inputPosition)
		local jumpRadius = jumpBtn.AbsoluteSize.x/2
		local centerPosition = getCenterPosition()
		local direction = Vector2.new(inputPosition.x - centerPosition.x, inputPosition.y - centerPosition.y)
		
		if direction.magnitude > jumpRadius then
			local angle = ATAN2(direction.y, direction.x)
			local octant = (FLOOR(8 * angle / (2 * PI) + 8.5)%8) + 1
			movementVector = COMPASS_DIR[octant]
		end
		
		if not flBtn.Visible and movementVector == COMPASS_DIR[7] then
			flBtn.Visible = true
			frBtn.Visible = true
		end
	end
	
	DPadFrame.InputBegan:connect(function(inputObject)
		if TouchObject or inputObject.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			return
		end
		
		MasterControl:AddToPlayerMovement(-movementVector)
		
		TouchObject = inputObject
		normalizeDirection(TouchObject.Position)
		
		MasterControl:AddToPlayerMovement(movementVector)
	end)
	
	DPadFrame.InputChanged:connect(function(inputObject)
		if inputObject == TouchObject then
			MasterControl:AddToPlayerMovement(-movementVector)
			normalizeDirection(TouchObject.Position)
			MasterControl:AddToPlayerMovement(movementVector)
			MasterControl:SetIsJumping(false)
		end
	end)
	
	OnInputEnded = function()
		TouchObject = nil
		flBtn.Visible = false
		frBtn.Visible = false
		
		MasterControl:AddToPlayerMovement(-movementVector)
		movementVector = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	end
	
	DPadFrame.InputEnded:connect(function(inputObject)
		if inputObject == TouchObject then
			OnInputEnded()
		end
	end)
	
	GuiService.MenuOpened:connect(function()
		if TouchObject then
			OnInputEnded()
		end
	end)
	
	DPadFrame.Parent = parentFrame
end

return DPad
