=== chunk=595 type=PROP offset=398582 runSha256=20fcbcce2207ca02ffc3aa4e126176059fbc58999dde7cb13e7fcde5d856c73d ===
local mp = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")

--inputs is a table of all inputs where ["InputName"] = value

--[[
	STATUSES:
	integer = good to load this item! (the int designates the ID offset to the Image)
	"invalid" = invalid ID
	"admin" = can't use an admin's hat
	"noload" = don't load this item (empty input)
	
--]]

local handling = {} --stores the players who are currently being managed, so we don't overload the system (15 second buffer)

function getProductInfo(id)
	local asset
	pcall(function() asset = mp:GetProductInfo(id) end)
	return asset
end

function addToHandling(plr)
	table.insert(handling,plr.Name)
	wait(12)
	table.remove(handling,1)
end

function canHandlePlayer(plr)
	local count = 0
	for i,v in pairs(handling) do
		if v == plr.Name then
			count = count + 1
		end
	end
	if count > 1 then return false end
	coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(addToHandling),plr)
	return true
end

function GetDataStoreSafeString(str)
	if not str then return end
	for i=1,#str do
		local letter = string.sub(str,i,i)
		if string.byte(letter) > 127 then
			return str:match("%w+") or "Invalid Name"
		end
	end
	return str
end

function GetFilteredNames(player, outfitname, rpname, rpdesc)
	local flag
	pcall(function()
		flag = game:GetService("Chat"):CanUserChatAsync(player.userId)
	end)
	if not flag then
		outfitname = "Saved Outfit"
		rpname = ""
		rpdesc = ""
		return outfitname, rpname, rpdesc
	end
	outfitname = GetDataStoreSafeString(outfitname)
	rpname = GetDataStoreSafeString(rpname)
	rpdesc = GetDataStoreSafeString(rpdesc)
	
	if outfitname and outfitname ~= "" then
		local filteredname
		pcall(function()
			filteredname = game:GetService("Chat"):FilterStringAsync(outfitname, player, player)
		end)
		if filteredname then
			if filteredname ~= outfitname then
				outfitname = "Saved Outfit"
			end
		end
	end
	
	if rpname and rpname ~= "" then
		for i=1,#rpname do
			local letter = string.sub(rpname,i,i)
			if letter:match("%A") and letter ~= " " and letter ~= "." then
				rpname = "INVALID_NAME"
			end
		end
		
		if not rpname:match("%a") then
			rpname = "INVALID_NAME"
		end
		
		if rpname ~= "INVALID_NAME" then
			local filteredname
			pcall(function()
				filteredname = game:GetService("Chat"):FilterStringAsync(rpname, player, player)
			end)
			
			if filteredname then
				if filteredname ~= rpname then
					if player then
						rpname = player.Name:match("%a+")
					end
					if rpname == nil then
						rpname = "Charlie"
					end
					rpname = string.sub(rpname,1,15)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	
	if rpdesc and rpdesc ~= "" then
		local pattern = [[

]]
		
		if rpdesc:match(pattern) then
			rpdesc = "INVALID_NAME"
		end
		
		if rpdesc ~= "INVALID_NAME" then
			local filteredname
			pcall(function()
				filteredname = game:GetService("Chat"):FilterStringAsync(rpdesc, player, player)
			end)
			if filteredname then
				rpdesc = filteredname
			end
		end
	end
	
	return outfitname, rpname, rpdesc
end

game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.GetFilteredNamesForOutfit.OnInvoke = function(plr,name,rpname,rpdesc)
	return GetFilteredNames(plr,name,rpname,rpdesc)
end


function checkOutfit(plr,inputs,wearonly) --don't check outfit name if wearonly == true
	local statuses = {}
	for i,v in pairs(inputs) do
		statuses[i] = "invalid" --give us a fresh table of the input names w/ statuses
	end
	
	inputs["OutfitName"], inputs["RPName"], inputs["RPDesc"] = GetFilteredNames(plr, inputs["OutfitName"], inputs["RPName"], inputs["RPDesc"])
	
	for i,v in pairs(inputs) do
		if i == "OutfitName" then
			if v == nil or ((v == "" or #v > 25) and not wearonly) or v == "INVALID_NAME" then
				statuses[i] = "invalid"
			else
				statuses[i] = 0
			end
		elseif i == "RPName" then
			if v == nil or (inputs["RPDesc"] ~= "" and inputs["RPName"] == "") or v == "INVALID_NAME" then
				statuses["RPName"] = "invalid"
			else
				statuses[i] = 0
			end
		elseif i == "RPDesc" then
			if v == nil or v == "INVALID_NAME" then
				statuses[i] = "invalid"
			else
				statuses[i] = 0
			end
		elseif i == "RemoveShirt" then
			statuses[i] = 0
		elseif v == "" then
			statuses[i] = "noload"
		elseif tostring(v):match("%D") then
			statuses[i] = "invalid"
		else --gettin' to the beefy stuff
			local asset = getProductInfo(v)
			if asset then
				--local creator = asset.Creator["Name"] --the creator's username
				
				if i:match("Hat") then
					if asset.AssetTypeId == 8 or (asset.AssetTypeId >= 41 and asset.AssetTypeId <= 47) then
						if (asset.AssetId ~= 72082328 or plr.Name == "Cindering") and (asset.AssetId ~= 62679779 or plr.Name ~= "Ultimateguy") and asset.AssetId ~= 1527622 and asset.AssetId ~= 1055299 then
							statuses[i] = 0
						elseif asset.AssetId == 501960266 and not (plr:FindFirstChild("HasRHSToy") and plr.HasRHSToy.Value == true) then --Has RHS Headphones
							statuses[i] = "invalid"
							game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr, "Earn the exclusive RHS Headphones item by purchasing a Roblox High School physical toy pack! Visit roblox.com/toys to learn more.", true)
						else
							statuses[i] = "admin"
						end
					else
						statuses[i] = "invalid"
					end
				elseif i == "Shirt" or i == "Pants" then
					if asset.AssetTypeId == 1 then --it's already an Image
						statuses[i] = 0
					elseif asset.AssetTypeId == 11 or asset.AssetTypeId == 12 then
						local count = 0
						local imgasset
						local success
						repeat
							pcall(function() imgasset = mp:GetProductInfo(v-(count+1)) end)
							if imgasset and imgasset.AssetTypeId == 1 then
								success = true
							else
								imgasset = nil
							end
							count = count + 1
						until success == true or count > 4
						if success == true then
							statuses[i] = count
						else
							statuses[i] = "invalid"
						end
					else
						statuses[i] = "invalid"
					end
				elseif i == "Face" then
					if asset.AssetTypeId == 18 then
						local textureid
						local faceasset
						pcall(function() faceasset = game:GetService("InsertService"):LoadAsset(v) textureid = (faceasset:GetChildren()[1].Texture):match("%d+") end)
						if faceasset then
							faceasset:Destroy()
						end
						if textureid then
							statuses[i] = v - tonumber(textureid)
						end
					end
				end
				
			else
				statuses[i] = "invalid"
			end
			
		end
	end
	
	return statuses
	
end

function productCheck(plr,slot)
	return true
end


game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.SaveOutfit.OnServerInvoke = function(plr,inputs,slot)
	if canHandlePlayer(plr) then
		local statuses = checkOutfit(plr,inputs) --returns statuses
		local valid = true
		for i,v in pairs(statuses) do
			v = tostring(v)
			if not v:match("%d") and not v:match("noload") then
				valid = false
			end
		end
		
		if valid then
			
			local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
			if data then
				local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(slot)
				if outfit and productCheck(plr,slot) == true then
					for i,v in pairs(inputs) do
						outfit[i].Value = v
					end
					return true
				end
			end
			
		else
			return false,statuses
		end
		
	end
end

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.WearOutfit.OnServerInvoke = function(plr,inputs)
	if canHandlePlayer(plr) then
		local statuses = checkOutfit(plr,inputs,true)
		local valid = true
		for i,v in pairs(statuses) do
			v = tostring(v)
			if not v:match("%d") and not v:match("noload") then
				valid = false
			end
		end
		
		if valid and plr and plr.Character then
			
			
			local hatstoadd = 0
			for i,v in pairs(inputs) do
				if i:match("Hat") and v ~= "" then
					hatstoadd = hatstoadd + 1
				end
			end
			for i,v in pairs(plr.Character:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("Accoutrement") then
					hatstoadd = hatstoadd + 1
				end
			end
			if hatstoadd > 3 then
				for i,v in pairs(plr.Character:GetChildren()) do
					if v:IsA("Accoutrement") then
						v:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end
			--begin loading assets
			for i,v in pairs(inputs) do
				if tostring(statuses[i]):match("%d") then --valid, load it
					if i:match("Hat") then
						local obj = game:GetService("InsertService"):LoadAsset(v)
						for _,hat in pairs(obj:GetChildren()) do
							if hat:IsA("Accoutrement") then
								hat.Parent = plr.Character
							end
						end
						obj:Destroy()
					elseif i == "RemoveShirt" then
						if v == true then
							for _,s in pairs(plr.Character:GetChildren()) do
								if s:IsA("Shirt") then
									s:Destroy()
								end
							end
						end
					elseif i == "Shirt" then
						for _,s in pairs(plr.Character:GetChildren()) do
							if s:IsA("Shirt") then
								s:Destroy()
							end
						end
						local shirt = Instance.new("Shirt",plr.Character)
						shirt.ShirtTemplate = "rbxassetid://"..tonumber(v) - statuses[i]
					elseif i == "Pants" then
						for _,s in pairs(plr.Character:GetChildren()) do
							if s:IsA("Pants") then
								s:Destroy()
							end
						end
						local pants = Instance.new("Pants",plr.Character)
						pants.PantsTemplate = "rbxassetid://"..tonumber(v) - statuses[i]
					elseif i == "Face" and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
						local face = plr.Character.Head:FindFirstChild("face")
						if face then
							face.Texture = "rbxassetid://"..tonumber(v) - statuses[i]
						end
					elseif i == "RPName" then
						local name = v
						local desc = inputs["RPDesc"]
						game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.ChangeRPNameFromOutfit:Fire(plr,name,desc)
					end
				end
			end
			
			return true

			
		else
			return false,statuses
		end
		
	end
end

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ChangeBodyMorph.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr,morphname)
	if plr.Character then
		for i,v in pairs(plr.Character:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("CharacterMesh") then
				v:Destroy()
			end
		end
		
		local group = script:FindFirstChild(morphname)
		if group then
			for i,v in pairs(group:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("CharacterMesh") then
					local clonemesh = v:clone()
					clonemesh.Parent = plr.Character
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)





=== chunk=595 type=PROP offset=408649 runSha256=f224ba46dad825031edc9b85aa82c632756e88c802a90fcca89d389db5fdad2c ===
local playerprizetables = {}

math.randomseed(tick())

local dss = game:GetService("DataStoreService")

function AddItemToData(plr,id)
	game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AwardItem:Fire(plr,id)
end


function GetItems(plr,type,minprice,maxprice) --return all possible items that can be awarded - if 0, award furniture instead
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if data then
		local possibleitems = {}
		for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name:match(type.."_") and v.ItemCost.Value >= minprice and v.ItemCost.Value <= maxprice and not data.Objects:FindFirstChild(v.ItemID.Value) and not v:FindFirstChild("NotForSale") and not v:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") and not (v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and v.ReleaseTime.Value > game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value) and not (v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") and v.TimeToGoOffSale.Value < game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value) then
				table.insert(possibleitems,v)
			end
		end
		return possibleitems
	end
end


--all these functions return the item's full table for going into the prize table


function GetFurniture(plr,minprice,maxprice,tier) --select random furniture based on price, and return table
	local possibleitems = {}
	for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name:match("furni_") and v.ItemCost.Value >= minprice and v.ItemCost.Value <= maxprice and not v:FindFirstChild("NotForSale") and not v:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") then
			table.insert(possibleitems,v)
		end
	end
	local item = possibleitems[math.random(1,#possibleitems)]
	local tbl = {
		["ItemName"] = string.sub(item.Name,7),
		["ItemID"] = item.ItemID.Value,
		["ImageID"] = "http://www.roblox.com/Thumbs/Asset.ashx?width=75&height=75&assetId=".. item.ModelID.Value,
		["PrizeTier"] = tier,
		["IsCash"] = false
	}
	return tbl
end

function GetPermanentItem(plr,item,tier) --return table for perm. gear/pets
	local tbl = {
		["ItemName"] = string.sub(item.Name,6),
		["ItemID"] = item.ItemID.Value,
		["ImageID"] = "rbxassetid://"..item.DecalID.Value,
		["PrizeTier"] = tier,
		["IsCash"] = false
	}
	return tbl
end

function GetCash(plr,amount,tier) --return table for cash amt
	local imgid = (tier == 3 and "rbxassetid://343339178" or tier == 4 and "rbxassetid://343339188" or "rbxassetid://343339162")
	local rareitemname
	if tier == 4 then
		rareitemname = "$100,000 Cash Jackpot!"
	end
	local tbl = {
		["ItemName"] = rareitemname or "+$"..amount.." RHS Cash",
		["ItemID"] = amount,
		["ImageID"] = imgid,
		["PrizeTier"] = tier,
		["IsCash"] = true
	}
	return tbl
end


--[[format of spin prize table:
	{  {"ItemName" = "Generic Item Name", "ItemID" = 210, "ImageID" = "rbxassetid://12345678", "PrizeTier" = 1, "IsCash" = false}, [...]  }
	if IsCash, then ItemID is the amount
--]]


--[[
Wheel Prizes:
	Mid-tier furniture (220-520) (COMMON)
	250-400 RHS Cash ($25 increments) (COMMON) (less likely to appear on wheel)
	Higher-tier furniture (500-900) (UNCOMMON) (most common)
	Gear (150-900) (UNCOMMON) (less likely to appear on wheel)
	Pets (250-1200) (UNCOMMON) (hardly ever on wheel)
	Rare furniture (Void, expensive goldens) (RARE)
	High-tier gear (1000-3000) (RARE)
	1000-2500 RHS Cash ($250 increments) (RARE) (unlikely to appear on wheel)
	$100,000 RHS Cash Jackpot (ULTRA-RARE)
--]]



function GetPrize(plr,tier)
	if tier == 1 then
		
		local chance = math.random(1,11)
		if chance <= 7 then --furniture 200-520
			return GetFurniture(plr,200,520,tier)
		else --cash 250-400 ($25 incs.)
			return GetCash(plr,225 + 25 * math.random(1,9),tier)
		end
		
	elseif tier == 2 then
		
		local chance = math.random(1,21)
		local possiblepets = GetItems(plr,"pets",250,1200)
		local possiblegear = GetItems(plr,"gear",150,900)
		if chance >= 12 and chance <= 18 and #possiblegear > 0 then
			return GetPermanentItem(plr,possiblegear[math.random(1,#possiblegear)],tier)
		elseif chance >= 19 and chance <= 21 and #possiblepets > 0 then
			return GetPermanentItem(plr,possiblepets[math.random(1,#possiblepets)],tier)
		else
			return GetFurniture(plr,500,950,tier)
		end
		
	elseif tier == 3 then
		
		local chance = math.random(1,10)
		local possiblegear = GetItems(plr,"gear",1000,3000)
		if chance >= 5 and chance <= 9 and #possiblegear > 0 then
			return GetPermanentItem(plr,possiblegear[math.random(1,#possiblegear)],tier)
		elseif chance == 10 then
			return GetCash(plr,750 + 250 * math.random(1,7),tier)
		else
			return GetFurniture(plr,1000,4200,tier)
		end
		
	elseif tier == 4 then
		return GetCash(plr,100000,tier)
	end
end

local numberofprizes = {9,6,2,1} --each prize tier

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.FetchSpinPrizeTable.OnServerInvoke = function(plr) --generate a player prize table, insert it into this server-side table, then send it to the client to use
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if data and data:FindFirstChild("LoyaltyStats") and data.LoyaltyStats.Spins.Value > 0 then
		local prizetable = {}
		for i,v in pairs(numberofprizes) do
			for x = 1,v do --pick out prizes for this tier
				table.insert(prizetable,GetPrize(plr,i))
			end
		end
		playerprizetables[plr.Name] = prizetable
		for x,tb in pairs(prizetable) do
			print("TABLE "..x)
			for i,v in pairs(tb) do
				print(i,v)
			end
			print("----------------------------------------")
		end
		return prizetable
	end
end

function WaitForWheelStop(plr,itemtable)
	coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
		local enabled = true
		local connection
		connection = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PrizeWheelStopped.OnServerEvent:connect(function(firer)
			local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
			if firer == plr and enabled and data then
				enabled = false
				connection:disconnect()
				if itemtable["IsCash"] == false then
					AddItemToData(plr,itemtable["ItemID"])
				else
					data.cash.Value = data.cash.Value + itemtable["ItemID"]
					
					--analytics
					if itemtable["ItemID"] == 100000 then
						game.ServerStorage.AnalyticsCache.CashEarned.Jackpot.Value = game.ServerStorage.AnalyticsCache.CashEarned.Jackpot.Value + 100000
					else
						game.ServerStorage.AnalyticsCache.CashEarned.PrizeSpin.Value = game.ServerStorage.AnalyticsCache.CashEarned.PrizeSpin.Value + itemtable["ItemID"]
					end
				end
				print("Successfully awarded prize "..itemtable["ItemName"]..", ID "..itemtable["ItemID"]..", to "..plr.Name)
			end
		end)
	end))
end

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.FetchWinningSpinPrize.OnServerInvoke = function(plr) --randomly select a winning prize from the player's prize table, then send the winning index back to the client - reduce player's spins by 1
	local tbl = playerprizetables[plr.Name]
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if tbl ~= nil and data and data:FindFirstChild("LoyaltyStats") and data.LoyaltyStats.Spins.Value > 0 then
		playerprizetables[plr.Name] = nil
		local tierchance = math.random(1,10000)
		local tier = 1
		if tierchance <= 5200 then
			tier = 1
		elseif tierchance >= 5201 and tierchance <= 9350 then
			tier = 2
		elseif tierchance >= 9351 and tierchance <= 9999 then
			tier = 3
		elseif tierchance == 10000 then
			tier = 4
		end
		print("Tier Chance: "..tierchance.." ... Prize Tier: "..tier)
		
		local availableindicies = {}
		
		for i,itemtable in pairs(tbl) do
			if itemtable["PrizeTier"] == tier then
				table.insert(availableindicies,i)
			end
		end
		
		data.LoyaltyStats.Spins.Value = data.LoyaltyStats.Spins.Value - 1
		
		local index = availableindicies[math.random(1,#availableindicies)] --winner!
		--now a function that will wait for the client's wheel to stop spinning to award the prize
		WaitForWheelStop(plr,tbl[index])
		
		print("Index "..index.." selected, player will win "..tbl[index]["ItemName"])
		return index
	end
endd
